Model before rewriting:

find x: tuple (int(1..2), int(1..5))
find y: tuple (int(2..5), int(1..5))

such that

x != y

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#packed_1: int(0..9)

--

x != y
   ~~> uniform_repr_in_comparison_op ([("ReprGeneral", 10100)])
x != y
new variables:
  find y#packed_1: int(0..19)

--

x != y
   ~~> select_representation_unconstrained ([("ReprGeneral", 9900)])
x != y
new variables:
  find x#packed_1#rank_1: bool
  find x#packed_1#rank_2: bool

  find x#packed_1#rank_4: bool

  find y#packed_1#rank_2: bool
  find y#packed_1#rank_3: bool
  find y#packed_1#rank_4: bool
  find y#packed_1#rank_5: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#packed_1#rank_4),and([!(x#packed_1#rank_3),and([!(x#packed_1#rank_2),or([!(x#packed_1#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  or([!(y#packed_1#rank_5),and([!(y#packed_1#rank_4),and([!(y#packed_1#rank_3),or([!(y#packed_1#rank_2),or([!(y#packed_1#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

x != y,
or([!(x#packed_1#rank_4),and([!(x#packed_1#rank_3),and([!(x#packed_1#rank_2);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),
or([!(y#packed_1#rank_5),and([!(y#packed_1#rank_4),and([!(y#packed_1#rank_3);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
x != y,
or([!(y#packed_1#rank_5),and([!(y#packed_1#rank_4),and([!(y#packed_1#rank_3);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(or([!(x#packed_1#rank_4),and([!(x#packed_1#rank_3),and([!(x#packed_1#rank_2);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

   ~~> tuple_packed_var_eq_var ([("ReprTuplePacked", 9700)])
